**Derived and non-authoritative.** The normative source is the owner-approved
`Governance_OS_Capability_Acceptance_Contract_v3.md` (SHA-256 `4c2df29115c8d5389034b2e2d817772add80d61678807c23e0d520c937cb5ed3`), imported byte-identically to

This file is generated by `gov contract compile` and checked by `gov contract verify`, which fails on any difference

below is the owner's text, verbatim, followed by its id and source line.

Universe: 101 capabilities in 23 gates; 713 checklist items; 8 advanced-qualification challenges.

### A1. Canonical authority and policy precedence

Source: `framework/contracts/source/GOVERNANCE_CAPABILITY_ACCEPTANCE_CONTRACT_v3.md:132` (lines 132–140) · requirement class `ORIGINAL` (no label in the source)

- [ ] Constitution/hard invariants are identifiable and machine-readable. <sub>A1.1 · L133</sub>
- [ ] Security and authority floors cannot be weakened by lower-precedence policy. <sub>A1.2 · L134</sub>
- [ ] Project policy may specialise/strengthen only within allowed override modes. <sub>A1.3 · L135</sub>
- [ ] Active decision/spec/task context follows deterministic precedence. <sub>A1.4 · L136</sub>
- [ ] Retrieved context/model inference cannot override higher authority. <sub>A1.5 · L137</sub>
- [ ] Invalid weakening attempts fail closed and are observable. <sub>A1.6 · L138</sub>

**Advanced qualification challenge:** inject contradictory project policies, obsolete provider rules, active/superseded decisions, malicious exceptions, and attempted authority/sensitivity weakening. <sub>AQC-A1 · L140</sub>

### A2. Authentic root of trust

Source: `framework/contracts/source/GOVERNANCE_CAPABILITY_ACCEPTANCE_CONTRACT_v3.md:142` (lines 142–154) · requirement class `POST_VERIFICATION_HARDENING` · source label `POST-VERIFICATION HARDENING` (line 142)

- [ ] Release/source authenticity is established before any privileged kernel material is staged or installed. <sub>A2.1 · L143</sub>
- [ ] Integrity and authenticity are treated as separate properties. <sub>A2.2 · L144</sub>
- [ ] Init/adopt/update/reinstall/recovery/rollback ingress paths share a non-circular trust root. <sub>A2.3 · L145</sub>
- [ ] Installed-kernel verification detects post-install tampering. <sub>A2.4 · L146</sub>
- [ ] Source-release authentication detects pre-install tampering. <sub>A2.5 · L147</sub>
- [ ] A source directory cannot regenerate its own trusted identity. <sub>A2.6 · L148</sub>
- [ ] Trusted release identity is recorded, not invented, by `framework.lock`. <sub>A2.7 · L149</sub>
- [ ] Bootstrap/dev/test trust modes cannot masquerade as certified production. <sub>A2.8 · L150</sub>
- [ ] Signing keys/trust anchors support rotation/revocation/recovery. <sub>A2.9 · L151</sub>
- [ ] Release verification works offline after obtaining an authentic release envelope where designed. <sub>A2.10 · L152</sub>

**Advanced qualification challenge:** tampered source payload, stale/forged manifest, wrong key, altered security policy, modified migration, downgrade/replay attempt, interrupted install. <sub>AQC-A2 · L154</sub>

### W10. Deterministic mandatory inputs outrank retrieval

Source: `framework/contracts/source/GOVERNANCE_CAPABILITY_ACCEPTANCE_CONTRACT_v3.md:1162` (lines 1162–1171) · requirement class `ORIGINAL` (no label in the source)

Hard invariant: <sub>L1163 · lead_in</sub>

> Mandatory task inputs are resolved through authoritative structured dependency/state mechanisms before supplementary retrieval. Semantic/lexical/graph/code retrieval may enrich the task but may not substitute for a required authoritative input. <sub>L1164 · hard_invariant</sub>

Verify: <sub>L1166 · lead_in</sub>

- [ ] current spec is delivered even if absent from semantic index <sub>W10.1 · L1167</sub>
- [ ] superseded but semantically similar spec cannot replace current required spec <sub>W10.2 · L1168</sub>
- [ ] token pressure drops supplementary context before mandatory authoritative inputs <sub>W10.3 · L1169</sub>
- [ ] retrieval/index outage does not erase deterministic required dependencies <sub>W10.4 · L1170</sub>
- [ ] required-input delivery is independently testable <sub>W10.5 · L1171</sub>

### W12. Health-scheduler integration

Source: `framework/contracts/source/GOVERNANCE_CAPABILITY_ACCEPTANCE_CONTRACT_v3.md:1185` (lines 1185–1194) · requirement class `ORIGINAL` (no label in the source)

- [ ] G0 blocks invalid authority/current-version substitution on privileged operations. <sub>W12.1 · L1186</sub>
- [ ] G1 invalidates affected dependency/lineage evidence after material mutations. <sub>W12.2 · L1187</sub>
- [ ] G2 verifies task input/consumption/traceability before task close. <sub>W12.3 · L1188</sub>
- [ ] G3 verifies mandatory-input continuity across checkpoint/handoff. <sub>W12.4 · L1189</sub>
- [ ] G4 runs wider staleness/impact propagation after CIT/spec/decision/architecture changes. <sub>W12.5 · L1190</sub>
- [ ] G5 audits end-to-end lineage and orphan states. <sub>W12.6 · L1191</sub>
- [ ] G6 injects hidden artifact-flow failures in sophisticated qualification. <sub>W12.7 · L1192</sub>

**Advanced qualification challenge:** intentionally omit current specs from semantic index, index superseded specs with higher semantic similarity, change specs after implementation, move files while retaining stable IDs, omit task dependency declarations, return code without consumption receipts, create contradictory mandatory inputs, induce context pressure, switch model/session, and create orphan authoritative outputs. The hidden oracle defines the correct required input/version and expected downstream propagation. <sub>AQC-W12 · L1194</sub>
